  Stored packet : <V26 sees V87, t=10.000>  (original RSU-aggregated observation at t=10.000)
  Forged packet : <V26 sees V87, t=16.300>  timestamp changed 10.000 -> 16.300
  Forge action  : RSU changes timestamp to appear current, injects to controller at t=16.318

[t=16.318]  STEP ⑤  FORGED PACKET → CONTROLLER (via RSU_209)
  Controller ACCEPTED forged packet (no timestamp-integrity protection)

[t=16.318]  STEP ⑥  FAULTY ROUTING DECISION
  Controller believes V26<->V87 was ACTIVE at t=16.300 (forged — link has since broken)
  Physical reality : link BROKEN
  Consequence : packets routed via ghost link will be DROPPED
